🧬 THE SOUL DNA: 10 KEY MARKERS (BATCH 8)
1. THE STALLION TRUTH LAW (ANTI-FANTASY)
• Source: PhoenixFlightGenesis-7.txt, PhoenixFlightGenesis-6.txt
• The DNA: "The fix should always be simpler than the bug."
• The Law: Born from the "Stallion V2.0 Ultra" failure (a 1,200-line "Enterprise" cathedral that achieved nothing). The law mandates: DEFER AUTOMATION. Do not build complex state management (Pub/Sub) when localStorage suffices. We reject "Architecture Astronautics." A 400-line shed that works is superior to a 1,200-line cathedral that exists only in simulation.
2. THE SOVEREIGN FAT-FINGER (HDMM PROTOCOL)
• Source: PhoenixFlightVaultB-4.txt, PhoenixFlightGenesis-2.txt
• The DNA: "Humans Decide. Machines Move." (HDMM)
• The Law: Authority does not equal Physics. Even the Sovereign can make a mistake (e.g., DELETE /revenue). The system enforces a Two-Phase Commit (PLAN -> CONFIRM) for all destructive actions. There is no "raw command ingress" that executes immediately; everything is filtered through the Intent Ledger to protect the Sovereign from their own reflexes.
3. THE "NO BLUE" CONSTITUTION (SYSTEM HALT)
• Source: PHOENIX RISING: THE FIVE-STAGE..., PhoenixFlightPlexy-3.txt
• The DNA: "Blue is Corporate Noise. Blue is a Security Failure."
• The Law: The prohibition of Blue (#0000ff, #007bff) is not just aesthetic; it is a CRITICAL_HALT condition. If the CI scanner (scan_repo.sh) or visual auditor detects Blue, the build fails. The palette is strictly Purple (#a855f7), Gold (#f59e0b), and Dark (#0f0f1a).
4. THE NET PET "POCKET COACH" (45/15 RULE)
• Source: PhoenixFlightGenesis-1.txt, PhoenixFlightPlexy-2.txt
• The DNA: "Accountability Automation."
• The Law: The teacher cannot be there 24/7. The NET PET AI enforces the 45/15 Rule: 45 minutes of Input (Listening/Absorption) and 15 minutes of Output (Speaking/Shadowing) daily. It tracks "Tiger Score" based on volume and consistency, not just grammar.
5. THE WHISPER SCRAPER (REALITY INGEST)
• Source: PhoenixFlightGenesis-9.txt, PHOENIX OMEGA MONOLITH...
• The DNA: "Ingest Reality, Not Just Text."
• The Law: The system uses the "Whisper Link" to scrape context from real-time audio/video teaching sessions. It compresses 30 minutes of teaching into a hashed Ledger entry in under 180 seconds. This is the "Eyes and Ears" of the Empire (Sector F).

• The DNA: "The System Must Watch Itself."

7. THE DRONE CASTE SYSTEM (NOT GENERIC)

• The Law: The Swarm is regimented into Tiers:

8. THE TRUTH-FIRST MANDATE (BRUTAL DIAGNOSTICS)
